### [1] [Scaling Inference Prefill with High-Radix Photonic Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01821)
*Arulselvan Madhavan,Peter Carson,Taylor Groves,Thomas Graham*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文量化了3D集成光子互连对MoE模型推理预填充阶段的性能提升，在高并发吞吐量场景下实现2.1-3.2倍延迟改进，在通信受限配置下实现2.8-5.8倍提升，并支持1152-GPU规模部署，实现2.2-4.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理成为当今主导的AI工作负载，日益复杂的混合专家（MoE）模型对大规模扩展提出更高要求。传统铜基互连面临带宽瓶颈，行业正向高带宽光子互连技术转型，亟需量化评估光子互连在推理预填充阶段的具体收益。

Method: 模拟三种MoE模型场景（短上下文1K-8K tokens、中等上下文128K tokens、长上下文1M tokens），在现有铜基GPU系统和集成光子互连系统上进行对比评估，分析高并发吞吐量与大上下文窗口之间的权衡关系。

Result: 在高批处理压力场景下实现2.1-3.2倍延迟改进；在通信受限配置下相比基线提升2.8-5.8倍；3D光子互连支持1152-GPU部署规模，在电气系统突破固有扩展限制时实现2.2-4.5倍加速。

Conclusion: 3D集成光子互连技术能够有效突破传统电气互连的扩展限制，显著降低时间到首个令牌（TTFT），为大语言模型推理和智能体AI应用提供更高的带宽和可扩展性支持。

Abstract: With the rise of inference as today's dominant AI workload, the industry is transitioning to high-bandwidth photonic interconnects to meet the large scale-up requirements of increasingly complex Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This paper quantifies the benefits of 3D-integrated photonic interconnects for inference prefill by analyzing tradeoffs between high-concurrency throughput for Large Language Model (LLM) chat and the large context windows typically required for reasoning and agentic AI. We simulate three MoE models: short context (1K--8K tokens), medium context (128K tokens), and long context (1M tokens). We evaluate this workload across existing copper-based GPU systems and one with high bandwidth integrated photonics. We show 2.1--3.2x latency improvements in the stressed high-batch regimes and 2.8--5.8x improvements over baselines in communication-limited configurations. 3D photonics enable the 1152-GPU footprint required to lower time-to-first-token, yielding 2.2--4.5x speedups across production-grade platforms when electrical systems cross their inherent scale-up-pod limits.

</details>


### [2] [CREDIT: Cost-guided Reduction-reuse with Efficient DSMEM Inter-CTA Tiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01864)
*Zhengxiong Li,Tsung-Wei Huang,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CREDIT框架，通过成本模型指导NVIDIA分布式共享内存(DSMEM)的优化应用，实现了91.7%的预测准确率，在RTX 5090和H100上分别获得1.466倍和1.318倍的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA分布式共享内存(DSMEM)支持线程块集群内的直接共享内存访问，但集群同步、远程访问和资源成本使得难以判断DSMEM何时能提升性能，缺乏有效的指导框架来识别和优化DSMEM适用的场景。

Method: CREDIT框架包含三个核心创新：(1)基于性能剖析的特征分析，识别可能受益于DSMEM的工作负载模式；(2)针对规约-复用工作负载的DSMEM变换技术；(3)基于剖析数据的成本模型，用于确定DSMEM的收益范围。

Result: 在多种工作负载上的评估显示：CREDIT在收益预测方面达到91.7%的准确率；在全部6个工作负载上均超越torch.compile、Triton和优化的非DSMEM CUDA基线，在RTX 5090上获得1.466倍几何平均加速，在H100上获得1.318倍几何平均加速。

Conclusion: CREDIT成功填补了DSMEM性能优化指导的空白，通过成本引导的方法实现了跨多种工作负载的一致性加速，证明了该方法在实际应用中的有效性和普适性。

Abstract: NVIDIA distributed shared memory (DSMEM) enables direct shared-memory access within a thread block cluster. However, cluster synchronization, remote access, and resource costs make it difficult to determine when DSMEM improves performance. To fill this gap, we propose CREDIT, a cost-guided framework that identifies DSMEM-profitable workload patterns, predicts their profitability range, and delivers consistent speedups across diverse workloads. CREDIT combines three innovations: (1) a profiling-driven characterization that identifies workload patterns likely to benefit from DSMEM; (2) a transformation that applies DSMEM to reduction-reuse workloads; (3) a cost model based on profiling data, to determine its profitability range. Evaluations on diverse workloads show CREDIT achieves 91.7% prediction accuracy on profitability. CREDIT beats torch.compile, Triton, and optimized non-DSMEM CUDA baselines on all six workloads, with geometric-mean speedups of 1.466x on RTX 5090 and 1.318x on H100. CREDIT's source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zhengxiongli08/CREDIT.

</details>


### [3] [RT-HiSS: Ray Tracing Accelerated High Dimensional Vector Similarity Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01975)
*Revanth Reddy Munugala,Michael Gowanlock*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了RT-HiSS，首个基于GPU光线追踪核心的高维数据精确相似性搜索算法，相比现有GPU算法最高加速8.37倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GPU光线追踪核心的相似性搜索方法不支持三维以上的数据集，且GPU相似性搜索在大数据集和大搜索距离下扩展性差。

Method: 利用RT核心进行快速索引构建和搜索，结合CUDA核心进行候选结果精炼；引入两阶段方法估算结果规模上界以实现内存限制下的高效批处理；采用共享内存分块和压缩结果掩码优化GPU资源利用。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上，相比竞争性GPU算法最高加速8.37倍，相比暴力搜索算法最高加速2368.26倍。

Conclusion: RT-HiSS成功将GPU光线追踪核心应用于高维数据相似性搜索，在保证精确性的同时显著提升了性能，证明了RT核心在通用计算任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent GPU generations include special-purpose ray tracing (RT) cores for graphics applications. While RT cores are primarily used for rendering, recent works show they can be leveraged for general-purpose tasks, including similarity searches. However, existing approaches do not support datasets exceeding three dimensions. In this work, we propose RT-HiSS, the first exact GPU RT-core-based similarity search algorithm for high-dimensional datasets. GPU similarity search often scales poorly for large datasets with substantial search distances. To address this, RT-HiSS uses RT cores for fast index construction and searches, followed by candidate refinement on CUDA cores. We introduce a two-pass approach to estimate an upper bound on result size, enabling efficient batching under GPU memory constraints with near-perfect load balancing. Additionally, we examine shared memory tiling and compressed result masks to improve GPU resource utilization. RT-HiSS yields speedups up to 8.37$\times$ over competitive state-of-the-art GPU algorithms and up to 2,368.26$\times$ relative to the brute-force algorithm across six real-world datasets.

</details>


### [4] [MeanField Surrogate Modeling for Scalable Runtime Scheduling of Concurrent Heterogeneous AI Inference on Shared GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02109)
*Youssef Ennouri,Soonhoi Ha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于平均场理论的代理模型，用于预测共享GPU上异构AI模型的性能，将采样成本从组合增长降低到近似线性增长，同时保持高预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享GPU上并发部署异构AI模型会引入资源竞争，导致运行时调度复杂化。现有的代理模型虽然避免了昂贵的在线基准测试，但其分析需求通常随并发运行模型数量呈组合增长，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出MeanField代理模型，通过局部配置和聚合GPU状态预测单个模型性能，而非显式建模所有联合交互关系。该模型避免了联合分析的组合复杂性，并与遗传算法调度器集成进行优化决策。

Result: 在N∈{2,3,4,5,6}的并发LLM和视觉工作负载实验中，模型达到R²≈0.96的高预测精度，经验样本预算随N近似线性增长。在N=5的问题（78,732个可行联合配置）中，遗传算法调度器达到穷举搜索0.10%以内的性能，在八个动态工作负载场景中零SLA违规，中位数决策时间26ms，比穷举代理搜索快约5倍。

Conclusion: MeanField代理模型成功解决了共享GPU上异构AI并发调度中的可扩展性挑战，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了分析成本，为大规模异构AI模型并发部署提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying heterogeneous AI models concurrently on a shared GPU introduces resource contention that complicates runtime scheduling. While surrogate models avoid costly online benchmarking, their profiling requirements typically grow combinatorially with the number of co-running models, limiting scalability. We propose a MeanField surrogate that predicts per-model performance from local configuration and aggregate GPU state rather than explicitly modeling all joint interactions. Experiments on concurrent LLM and vision workloads across $N \in \{2,3,4,5,6\}$ show high predictive accuracy ($R^2 \approx 0.96$) with an empirical sample budget that grows approximately linearly in $N$, in contrast to the combinatorial cost of fully joint profiling. Integrated into a genetic algorithm scheduler, the surrogate scales to an $N=5$ problem with 78,732 feasible joint configurations, remaining within 0.10% of the exhaustive search with zero SLA violations across eight dynamic workload scenarios, while complete online GA decisions take 26 ms median, about $5\times$ faster than exhaustive surrogate search.

</details>


### [5] [Towards Global Federated Genome-Wide Association Meta-Analysis Using GA4GH TES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02227)
*Abhijit Chunduru,Matthew Joel,Zilinghan Li,Ravi Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 基于GA4GH TES标准和联邦学习框架，开发了隐私保护的GWAS元分析流程，在不共享个体基因型数据的情况下实现了跨机构的全基因组关联研究，在10万模拟个体中验证了与集中式分析相当的统计效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全基因组关联研究(GWAS)需要大规模、祖先多样化的队列以获得足够统计效能，但隐私法规和数据驻留限制常常阻碍基因组数据跨机构或国界的集中汇总，亟需既能保护隐私又能实现大规模协作的分析方法。

Method: 基于APPFL框架构建隐私保护的联邦GWAS元分析流程，各站点计算本地GWAS汇总统计量，仅传输聚合结果；通过GA4GH任务执行服务(TES)端点全球网络实现计算向数据迁移；服务器执行逆方差加权固定效应元分析；开发HiveWatch地理可观测性工具包进行分布式任务实时监控。

Result: 在5个站点、10万合成个体和约24万个变异位点的2型糖尿病和BMI模拟研究中，联邦元分析成功复现了预期来自集中式分析的关联信号，且未集中任何基因型数据，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于标准的任务执行和联邦学习技术能够为国际GWAS元分析构建实用的隐私保护基础设施，在满足数据驻留和隐私合规要求的同时实现大规模基因组研究的统计效能。

Abstract: Genome-wide association studies (GWAS) gain statistical power from large, ancestrally diverse cohorts, but privacy regulations and data-residency constraints often prevent genomic data from being centrally pooled across institutional or national borders. We present a privacy-preserving federated GWAS meta-analysis pipeline built on the APPFL framework, in which each site computes local GWAS summary statistics and transmits only aggregate results, never individual-level genotypes. Analysis is executed through a global network of Global Alliance for Genomics and Health (GA4GH) Task Execution Service (TES) endpoints, which allows computation to move to the data rather than the reverse. The server performs inverse-variance-weighted fixed-effect meta-analysis and returns aggregated results to all sites, while HiveWatch, our developed geographic observability toolkit, provides real-time monitoring of distributed task execution. In a five-site simulation over 100,000 synthetic individuals and roughly 240,000 variants for Type 2 Diabetes and Body Mass Index, the federated meta-analysis reproduces the association signal expected from a pooled analysis without centralizing any genotype data, showing that standards-based task execution and federated learning enables a practical privacy-preserving infrastructure for international GWAS meta-analysis.

</details>


### [6] [Federated Learning on the American Science Cloud using APPFL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02238)
*Zilinghan Li,Abhijit Chunduru,Harinarayan Krishnan,Eric Chagnon,Peter Nugent,Kibaek Kim,Ravi Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在AmSC平台上部署APPFL框架的编排逻辑作为可扩展的云服务，以弥合当前平台在跨组织边界训练模型方面的能力缺失，实现隐私保护的科学协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国能源部的AmSC平台虽然提供了模型推理、实验跟踪和跨设施函数执行等服务，但缺乏在数据无法集中（由于政策、隐私或规模限制）的情况下进行跨组织边界模型训练的能力。这正是联邦学习（FL）的典型应用场景，也是科学AI领域的迫切需求。

Method: 在AmSC现有原语基础上部署高级隐私保护联邦学习（APPFL）框架的编排逻辑作为可扩展的云服务。具体利用AmSC的四项核心功能：支持安全可靠联邦成员身份的项目级认证、驱动各站点分布式训练的函数执行、记录轮级性能的实验跟踪，以及用于向授权参与者分发联邦训练模型的模型托管和推理基础设施。

Result: 成功将APPFL框架集成到AmSC平台，实现了跨组织边界的隐私保护联邦学习服务，填补了平台在分布式模型训练方面的能力空白。该方案无需修改现有基础设施，而是将其作为编排层叠加在已有原语之上。

Conclusion: 将联邦计算作为AmSC的重要服务，能够解锁受隐私限制的科学协作机会，支持公私合作构建模型，同时锻炼和增强平台自身的联邦基础设施能力。这为科学AI的发展提供了新的可能性，特别是在数据隐私和主权要求严格的场景下。

Abstract: The American Science Cloud (AmSC), established under the Genesis Mission of the U.S. Department of Energy (DOE), aims to integrate DOE high-performance computing systems, experimental facilities, and data resources into a single, coordinated, AI-driven discovery platform. AmSC's early services focus on curated artifacts, such as gated inference access to hosted models, experiment tracking, and function execution across computing facilities. However, what these services lack is a means to train a model across organizational boundaries where data cannot be centralized due to policy, privacy, or scale. This is, by definition, a use case for federated learning (FL) and a growing class of scientific AI. In this paper, we show that this gap can be bridged by deploying the orchestration logic of the Advanced Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning (APPFL) framework as a scalable cloud service on top of the primitives AmSC already provides: project-scoped authentication that supports secure and reliable federation membership, function execution that drives distributed training at each site, experiment tracking that records round-level performance, and finally, the model-hosting and inference infrastructure that can be leveraged to distribute the federated trained models to authorized participants. We argue that offering federated computing as an important AmSC service would unlock privacy-constrained scientific collaborations, enabling public-private partnerships in model building while exercising and enhancing the platform's own federated infrastructure.

</details>


### [7] [AceSpec: An Asymmetric Edge-Cloud Collaborative Framework for Communication-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02514)
*Yida Zhang,Zhiyong Gao,Shuaibing Yue,Jie Li,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出AceSpec框架，通过边缘-云端协作的推测解码解决WAN通信瓶颈问题，实现边缘设备上LLM的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘设备LLM部署方案存在明显缺陷：模型压缩损害推理能力，分割推理受WAN通信瓶颈限制。边缘-云端推测解码虽有前景，但在不稳定WAN环境下，预测拒绝会导致流水线停顿和全网回滚，抵消了协作增益。

Method: 提出非对称边缘-云端协作框架AceSpec。利用边缘计算资源预先构建概率状态缓存，将全网流水线刷新转化为O(1)本地内存查找；采用非对称通信协议，上行传输最小主链索引，下行传输紧凑稀疏分布以节省带宽；引入网络感知的拉格朗日优化资源分配策略，动态最大化本地缓存命中率。

Result: 实验显示，AceSpec实现最高3.52倍吞吐量加速，具有优异的带宽免疫能力。在严重受限的50 Kbps WAN条件下仍能维持接近峰值的推理性能。

Conclusion: AceSpec成功解决了边缘-云端推测解码在易变WAN环境下的关键挑战，为边缘设备LLM部署提供了一种高效、鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on edge devices typically relies on model compression or split inference. However, compression degrades reasoning capabilities, while split inference suffers from severe Wide Area Network (WAN) communication bottlenecks. Edge-cloud speculative decoding emerges as a promising alternative, leveraging an edge small model to draft tokens for cloud verification. Yet, over volatile WANs, inevitable prediction rejections trigger catastrophic pipeline stalls and network-wide rollbacks, neutralizing collaborative gains. To overcome this, we propose AceSpec, an asymmetric edge-cloud collaborative framework. AceSpec utilizes un-saturated edge compute to proactively construct a probabilistic state cache, effectively transforming network-wide pipeline flushes into $\mathcal{O}(1)$ local memory lookups. To preserve bandwidth, it employs an asymmetric communication protocol that transmits minimal main-chain indices uplink and compact sparse distributions downlink. Furthermore, we introduce a network-aware, Lagrangian-optimized resource allocation strategy that dynamically maximizes the local cache hit rate. Evaluations demonstrate that AceSpec achieves up to a 3.52$\times$ throughput speedup and exhibits exceptional bandwidth immunity, sustaining near-peak inference performance even under severely constrained 50 Kbps WAN conditions.

</details>

### [8] [RunSoC 2.0: Scheduling and Allocating Automotive Software Tasks to Hardware Partitions in Heterogeneous MPSoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01614)
*Daniel Krüger,Lucas Mauser,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: RunSoC 2.0是一个针对异构MPSoC的早期设计空间探索框架，用于汽车任务调度与分配的多目标优化，CP-SAT求解器表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式汽车架构将计算密集型工作负载整合到异构多处理器片上系统(MPSoC)上，产生了严格的执行、内存和通信约束。需要早期设计阶段的工具来探索任务调度和分配方案，以应对异构平台的复杂性。

Method: 基于RunSoC 1.0扩展，支持异构平台建模：处理器特定执行时间、集群级组织、领域特定处理属性。将任务集表示为有向无环图(DAG)，受端到端延迟和核心亲和性约束。将调度与分配建模为多目标优化问题，最小化层次化内存预算违规和核间/集群间通信代价。支持多种求解后端：CBC、CP-SAT和遗传算法(GA)。

Result: 在10到500个任务的合成汽车任务集上评估，映射到Renesas R-Car V4H、NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin和TI TDA4VM等异构MPSoC。CP-SAT在紧约束硬实时调度实例中持续优于CBC和GA。框架能生成可行且最优的调度方案，暴露架构瓶颈，支持平台替代方案的快速比较。

Conclusion: RunSoC 2.0通过集群感知通信和内存建模，提高了早期MPSoC分析的真实性，同时为大型汽车工作负载保持实用的求解时间，为异构MPSoC的设计决策提供有效支持。

Abstract: Centralized automotive architectures increasingly consolidate compute-intensive workloads onto heterogeneous Multi-Processor System-on-Chip (MPSoC), creating strict execution, memory, and communication constraints. This paper presents RunSoC 2.0, a customizable framework for early-stage design-space exploration of task scheduling and allocation on heterogeneous MPSoCs. Building on RunSoC 1.0, which targeted allocation on homogeneous hardware, RunSoC 2.0 extends the framework to heterogeneous platforms by modeling processor-specific execution times, cluster-level organization, and domain-specific processing properties. It represents task sets as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) subjected to strict end-to-end latency and core-affinity constraints, and formulates task scheduling and allocation as a multi-objective optimization problem that minimizes hierarchical memory-budget violations and inter-core/inter-cluster communication penalties. The framework supports multiple solving backends, including COIN-OR Branch and Cut (CBC), Google OR-Tools CP-SAT, and a Genetic Algorithm (GA), enabling comparative evaluation of exact, constraint-programming, and meta-heuristic approaches. We evaluate RunSoC 2.0 using synthetic automotive task sets ranging from 10 to 500 tasks, mapped to representative heterogeneous MPSoCs, including the Renesas R-Car V4H, NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, and TI TDA4VM. The results show that RunSoC 2.0 can generate feasible and optimal schedules, expose architectural bottlenecks, and support rapid comparison of platform alternatives. Notably, CP-SAT consistently outperforms both CBC and the GA across tightly constrained hard real-time scheduling instances. By incorporating cluster-aware communication and memory modeling, RunSoC 2.0 improves the realism of early-stage MPSoC analysis while retaining practical solution times for large automotive workloads. (..)

</details>


### [9] [An Emerging NVM-Based On-Chip Training Architecture with Non-Ideality Mitigation Through Bipolar Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01948)
*Peng Dang,Youna Huang,Yintao He,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA架构和非理想性规避训练（NAT）算法，解决了新兴非易失性存储器（eNVM）器件的非理想特性问题，在器件严重不对称情况下平均提升15.1%精度，并实现约33.58倍于GPU的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的快速发展对传统冯·诺伊曼架构提出了显著的能效挑战。虽然基于新兴非易失性存储器（eNVM）的存内计算（IMC）架构因其高并行性和低功耗被认为是加速神经网络训练的有前景的解决方案，但eNVM器件固有的非理想特性会导致电导更新偏离目标值，从而限制了片上训练的性能。

Method: 研究者首先制备了二维铁电场效应晶体管（FeFET），并开发了基于实验数据校准的电导调制行为模型。在此基础上，首次提出了针对eNVM器件的非理想性规避训练（NAT）算法，该算法通过引导权重收敛至eNVM器件最稳定的电导区域来缓解精度下降问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使在器件严重不对称的情况下，NAT算法在多个基准任务上相比基线方法平均提升了15.1%的精度。同时，NOVA架构相比GPU的峰值能效实现了约33.58倍的能效提升。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了面向片上训练的非理想性优化eNVM加速器（NOVA）架构，通过结合实验器件建模和创新的训练算法，有效解决了eNVM器件非理想特性对神经网络训练的影响，显著提升了训练精度和能效，为深度学习硬件加速器的发展提供了重要贡献。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of deep learning has presented significant energy efficiency challenges to the conventional von Neumann architecture. In-memory computing (IMC) architectures based on emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) are widely regarded as a promising solution for accelerating neural network training due to their high parallelism and low power consumption. However, the intrinsic non-idealities of eNVM devices can cause conductance updates to deviate from target values, thereby limiting the performance of on-chip training. To address this challenge, this paper presents a Non-ideality Optimized eNVM Accelerator (NOVA) architecture for on-chip training. Specifically, we first fabricate a two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric field-effect transistor (FeFET) and develop a conductance modulation behavioral model calibrated with experimental data. Building upon this device model, we propose, for the first time, a Non-ideality Avoidance Training (NAT) algorithm tailored for eNVM devices, which mitigates accuracy degradation by guiding weight convergence toward the most stable conductance regions of eNVM devices. Experimental results demonstrate that, even under severe device asymmetry, NAT improves the accuracy by an average of 15.1\% over the baseline methods across multiple benchmark tasks. Meanwhile, the NOVA achieves an average energy efficiency gain of approximately 33.58$\times$ compared with the peak energy efficiency of graphics processing units (GPUs).

</details>


### [10] [Atlas: Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for On-Device City-Scale 3D Gaussian Splatting in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02352)
*He Zhu,Zheng Liu,Xingyang Li,Anbang Wu,Zihan Liu,Ruyang Li,Hui Wei,Yaqian Zhao,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Atlas框架通过分层内存卸载机制实现移动VR设备上的城市规模3D高斯渲染，无需网络连接，性能提升18.5倍并节能92.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市规模3D高斯渲染（3DGS）的内存需求远超移动GPU容量，而现有移动VR设备无法处理大规模场景，亟需一种可在设备端高效运行的解决方案。

Method: 提出分层内存卸载机制，根据当前姿态和细节层次需求动态加载必要的高斯数据；引入时间感知的LoD搜索和立体光栅化技术，避免VR中的冗余计算；可与现有3DGS加速器集成。

Result: 相比GPU基线实现18.5倍加速，相比最先进的3DGS加速器实现3.9倍加速，能耗降低92.4%。

Conclusion: Atlas成功实现了移动VR设备上的城市规模3DGS渲染，无需运行时网络访问，为移动端大规模场景渲染提供了高效可行的技术方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has drawn significant attention in the architectural community recently. However, enabling city scale 3DGS on mobile VR devices remains challenging, as the memory requirement of large scale scenes far exceeds the memory capacity of today's mobile GPUs. This paper presents Atlas, an on device city scale 3DGS rendering framework that enables scalable rendering without runtime Internet access. The key insight is that although the full 3DGS model is massive, each frame only requires a small subset of Gaussians under the current pose and level of detail requirement. Based on this insight, Atlas introduces a hierarchical memory offloading mechanism that dynamically loads only necessary Gaussian data into device memory. To further improve performance, Atlas proposes temporal aware LoD search and stereo rasterization to avoid redundant computation in VR. We further show that our technique can be integrated with existing 3DGS accelerators with negligible hardware overhead. Overall, Atlas achieves 18.5x speedup over the GPU baseline and 3.9x speedup over the state of the art 3DGS accelerators, with 92.4% energy savings.

</details>


### [11] [Batch Before You Time: Decision-Scoped Proxy Execution for Timing-Aware Logic Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02470)
*Pujun Su*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BBYT方法通过批量编译候选重写来减少时序评估开销，平均减少18.05%的候选选择时间，同时保持与穷举时序选择相同的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准延迟格式(SDF)标注的开关仿真能区分功能等效重写中的延迟依赖活动，但评估每个候选需要重复进行时序分析、编译和回放。零延迟代理可消除时序评估，但逐候选生成代理的成本可能超过其节省的时序工作。

Method: 提出BBYT(Batch Before You Time)方法，将同一重写决策的所有候选编译到一个作用域零延迟镜像中，然后提交分离良好的代理胜者或调用不变的时序链，避免逐候选生成代理。

Result: 在12个平衡保留序列上，BBYT平均减少18.05%的完整候选选择时间，设计级减少5.84%至30.25%；在8192转换C6288工作负载上比逐候选代理启动快9.24%；在五工作负载语料库中移除32.52%的时序候选评估，且在所有250个评估决策上匹配穷举时序选择。

Conclusion: 对于按需时序感知重写选择，代理执行和保真度延续应使用相同的决策作用域，BBYT证明了批量处理候选可有效减少时序评估开销。

Abstract: Standard Delay Format (SDF)-annotated switching simulation distinguishes delay-dependent activity among functionally equivalent rewrites, but evaluating every candidate repeats timing, compilation, and replay. A zero-delay proxy can remove timed evaluations, but generating that proxy candidate by candidate can cost more than the timed work it saves. We present Batch Before You Time (BBYT), which compiles all candidates of one rewrite decision into one scoped zero-delay image and either commits a well-separated proxy winner or invokes the unchanged timed chain. Across 12 counterbalanced holdout sequences, BBYT reduces complete candidate-selection time by 18.05% on average; the design-level reductions are 5.84% and 30.25%, with both confidence intervals above zero. On a counterbalanced 8,192-transition C6288 workload, BBYT is 9.24% faster than the same gate executed with candidate-wise proxy launches. In the five-workload corpus, BBYT removes 32.52% of timed candidate evaluations and matches exhaustive timed selection on all 250 evaluated decisions. For on-demand timing-aware rewrite selection, proxy execution and fidelity continuation should use the same decision scope.

</details>

### [12] [Curriculum-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Energy-Efficient UAV-ISAC in Post-Disaster Search-and-Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01764)
*Tai-You Guo,Chuan-Chi Lai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种课程引导的软演员-评论家（CG-SAC）框架，用于无人机集成感知与通信系统的节能优化，在2000个随机场景中实现了0.72 Mbits/J的平均能效和66%-82%的飞行步数减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机是集成感知与通信（ISAC）的有前途平台，但其有限的机载能量导致感知精度、通信质量和推进成本之间存在强烈耦合关系，亟需一种能够平衡这些相互竞争目标的节能优化方法。

Method: 提出课程引导的软演员-评论家（CG-SAC）框架，采用推进感知奖励塑形技术，联合优化3D轨迹、通信-感知功率分配和每用户功率分配。引入旋翼推进模型推导闭式推进经济巡航速度，构建包含导航、节点访问、能效和约束惩罚项的归一化复合奖励，并使用对数线性课程在训练过程中逐步收紧通信、感知和接近度要求。

Result: 在2000个随机场景中，CG-SAC实现0.72 Mbits/J的平均能效，显著优于其他深度强化学习基线；在成功完成的任务中平均需要107.6步，相比基线减少66%-82%的飞行步数；在服务时刻实现99.6%的通信速率满足率；学习到的策略展现出任务感知的速度自适应能力，在服务点附近减速，在转场过程中加速。

Conclusion: CG-SAC框架通过推进感知奖励塑形和课程学习有效解决了无人机ISAC系统中的能效优化问题，学习到的策略能够自适应地调整飞行速度以平衡任务可行性和能源效率，各奖励组件在平衡任务可行性和能效方面发挥互补作用。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are promising platforms for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC), but their limited onboard energy creates a strong coupling among sensing accuracy, communication quality, and propulsion cost. This paper proposes a curriculum-guided soft actor-critic (CG-SAC) framework with propulsion-aware reward shaping for energy-efficient UAV-ISAC, jointly optimizing the 3D trajectory, communication-sensing power split, and per-user power allocation. A rotary-wing propulsion model is incorporated to derive a closed-form propulsion-economic cruising speed, which is used to construct a propulsion-aware speed-shaping term within a normalized composite reward together with navigation, node-visiting, energy-efficiency, and constraint-penalty terms. A log-linear curriculum progressively tightens the communication, sensing, and proximity requirements during training. Across 2000 randomized scenarios, CG-SAC achieves an average energy efficiency of 0.72 Mbits/J, substantially outperforming the evaluated DRL baselines. Among successfully completed missions, it requires 107.6 steps on average, corresponding to a 66%--82% reduction in flight steps relative to the baselines. Crucially, the learned policy exhibits mission-aware speed adaptation by decelerating near service points and accelerating during transit, while achieving a 99.6% communication-rate satisfaction ratio at service instants. Ablation results further demonstrate the complementary roles of the reward components in balancing mission feasibility and energy efficiency.

</details>


### [13] [Agents That Model Agents: Five Principles Toward a Theory of Mind for 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01779)
*Hatim Chergui,Carolina Fernández-Martínez,Mehdi Bennis,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于认知信道的韧性多智能体框架，通过心智建模(ToM)和层论分析解决LLM智能体间AI幻觉传播问题，在1B参数电信语言模型上验证了认知信噪比能隔离幻觉智能体，二层ToM恢复正确行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来6G网络将依赖大语言模型智能体管理无线接入网，但现有架构假设智能体间消息传递客观事实。实际上消息是发送者推理的主观结论痕迹，语法有效的报告可能传播AI幻觉并引发协议验证无法检测的级联故障。

Method: 将智能体交互建模为元胞束上的认知信道，建立韧性多智能体系统统一框架。提出五个设计原则：(1)消息是发送者隐藏推理的证据；(2)信任是连续的认知信噪比；(3)通过层拉普拉斯算子计算网络一致性；(4)对等建模精确止于二层；(5)可信容量受目标一致性约束。在本地部署的1B参数电信语言模型上进行信令风暴研究验证。

Result: 认知信噪比成功隔离了与四个邻居中三个达成一致的幻觉对等体；二层ToM恢复正确行动；谱间隙决定拓扑是否在近实时预算内达成一致性；而基于分歧的门控方法将所有错误对等体排在正确对等体之上。

Conclusion: 该框架为6G网络中LLM智能体系统提供理论基础和设计准则，证明了心智建模和层论方法在防御AI幻觉传播方面的有效性，为构建可靠的智能网络管理系统指明方向。

Abstract: Future 6G networks will rely on Large Language Model (LLM) agents to manage the Radio Access Network (RAN). However, current architectures assume inter-agent messages convey objective facts. A message is instead a \emph{trace} of the sender's reasoning: it carries a subjective conclusion, so a syntactically valid report can propagate an AI hallucination and trigger a cascading outage invisible to protocol validation. Reading such a trace requires a Theory of Mind (ToM)---before acting, the receiver must model what the peer believes, and what a peer in that position should have believed. Modeling these interactions as cognitive channels on a cellular sheaf, we obtain a unified framework for resilient multi-agent systems, from which five design principles emerge: (i) a message is evidence of the sender's hidden reasoning; (ii) trust is a continuous cognitive Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR)---asserted precision over deviation from the modeled peer belief; (iii) network-wide consistency and resistance to hallucination contagion are computable via the sheaf's Laplacian; (iv) peer-modeling must halt at exactly two levels to conserve compute and survive mutual information decay; and (v) credible capacity is bounded by operational goal alignment, not link bandwidth. A signaling-storm study on locally deployed 1B-parameter telecom language models validates it: cognitive SNR isolates a hallucinating peer that three of its four neighbors agree with, where a divergence gate ranks every wrong peer above the right one; only depth two ToM recovers the correct action; and the spectral gap decides whether a topology reaches consistency inside the near-real-time budget.

</details>


### [14] [Will there be a 7G?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01877)
*Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了7G是否存在的必要性问题，提出了一个包含需求驱动、系统级不连续性、协调价值、可持续性、信任和地缘政治可行性等维度的评估框架，用于判断7G是否应成为独立的移动代际、6G演进的延续或更广泛的后6G基础设施网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ITU-R IMT-2030框架确立6G愿景和3GPP Release 21定义6G规范路径，研究界面临一个关键问题：是否会有7G，如果有，其存在理由是什么？作者认为7G不应仅是不可避免的编号延续或更雄心勃勃的无线目标清单。

Method: 开发了一个就绪度评估框架，涵盖六个维度：需求驱动的需求、系统级不连续性、协调价值、可持续性和循环性、信任以及地缘政治可行性。然后将该框架应用于候选7G不连续性技术，包括代理化网络运营、射频原生计算、量子使能互操作、策略感知频谱治理、电网交互基础设施、结果保证服务和区域化标准。

Result: 通过框架分析，文章指出7G的正当性取决于后6G系统是否能引入6G/6G-Advanced、Wi-Fi、NTN、私有蜂窝、中立主机部署、边缘云平台或互补无线和软件系统无法满足的需求或协调问题。研究识别了多项潜在的7G不连续性候选技术。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献不是预测固定的7G架构，而是提供一个结构化的决策基础，用于判断7G是否应成为独立的移动代际、6G演进的延续或更广泛的后6G基础设施网络。这为未来移动通信发展提供了系统性的评估方法论。

Abstract: The transition from 5G to 6G is becoming concrete: the ITU-R IMT-2030 framework has established the high-level vision and capability set for 6G, while 3GPP Release 21 has defined the path toward the first 6G specifications. This raises a deliberately provocative question for the research and standards communities: will there be a 7G, and if so, what would justify it? This paper argues that 7G should not be treated as an inevitable numbering exercise or as a catalogue of more ambitious radio targets. Instead, its justification should depend on whether post-6G systems introduce needs or coordination problems that cannot be met by 6G/6G-Advanced, Wi-Fi, NTN, private cellular, neutral-host deployments, edge-cloud platforms, or complementary wireless and software-based systems. To support this assessment, the paper develops a readiness framework covering demand-led need, system-level discontinuity, coordination value, sustainability and circularity, trust, and geopolitical viability. It then applies the framework to candidate 7G discontinuities, including agentic network operation, RF-native computing, quantum-enabled interworking, policy-aware spectrum governance, grid-interactive infrastructure, outcome-assured services, and regionalized standards. The contribution is not a prediction of a fixed 7G architecture, but a structured basis for deciding whether 7G should become a distinct mobile generation, an extension of 6G evolution, or a broader post-6G infrastructure fabric.

</details>


### [15] [Network-Aware Forecasting on Wireless Access Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01957)
*Niloo Bahadori,Swadhin Pradhan,Peiman Amini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 企业无线AP适合运行预测性ML，但与网络服务存在资源竞争。研究提出网络感知部署框架，发现边缘测试平台无法准确反映真实AP性能（慢6.1-19.1倍，内存差异22%），且ML推理会显著影响网络性能（RTT增加76%，吞吐量降低7.06%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AP虽然是有前景的ML推理平台，但其主要职责仍是提供网络服务。ML推理与网络功能共享CPU和内存资源会引发两个风险：模型在代理硬件上测试良好但在目标AP上可能太慢；单独测试可行的模型在网络负载下可能降低服务质量。现有边缘测试平台无法可靠预测目标AP的实际行为。

Method: 定义"网络感知可部署性"框架，包含两个门控机制：首先验证模型及其执行路径在目标AP上的合格性，然后在满足数据包服务和预测约束的条件下验证其执行性能。通过基准测试对比边缘测试平台与真实AP的性能差异，测试5种模型实现和2个基础预测模型在不同服务配置下的表现。

Result: 边缘测试平台（Raspberry Pi 5）与目标AP性能差异显著：相同配置下模型在AP上运行慢6.1-19.1倍，峰值内存差异达22%。相似大小的预测基础模型AP延迟相差19倍。在网络饱和条件下运行较小模型处理13个并行流时，默认执行方式使P99往返时间增加76%，吞吐量降低7.06%。

Conclusion: 在AP上同时运行网络服务和ML工作负载需要深入理解这些权衡关系。边缘测试平台不能可靠预测目标AP行为，必须采用网络感知的部署验证方法。这些发现对实际部署至关重要，强调了在AP上进行ML推理时必须考虑其对网络服务性能的影响。

Abstract: Enterprise wireless access points (APs) are promising platforms for predictive machine learning (ML), but their primary responsibility remains providing wireless connectivity and network services. Predictive inference must therefore share an AP's CPU and memory with packet processing, Wi-Fi and IoT radio operations, and client management. This resource contention creates two risks: a model that performs well on proxy hardware may be too slow on the target AP, while a model that fits in isolation may still degrade network services under load. We define \textit{network-aware deployability} using two gates: qualification of the model and its execution path on the target AP, followed by validation of its execution profile under packet-service and forecasting constraints. Our benchmarks show that edge testbeds do not reliably capture target behavior. Across matched artifacts and serving settings, five model implementations run 6.1--19.1$\times$ slower on an AP than on a Raspberry Pi~5, while peak memory usage differs by up to 22\%. Moreover, two forecasting foundation models of similar size differ in AP latency by 19$\times$. When serving a smaller model across 13 parallel streams at a 30~s cadence under network saturation, default execution increases p99 round-trip time (RTT) by 76\% and reduces throughput by 7.06\%. Understanding these trade-offs is essential for live deployment if we aim to use APs for both networking and ML workloads.

</details>


### [16] [Bridging the Gap: A Longitudinal Analysis of Extended Identifiers in the Post-Cookie Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02069)
*Michael Smith,Riley Grossman,Krzysztof Franaszek,Antonio Torres-Agüero,Pritam Sen,Cristian Borcea,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究对145万个网站上的1.45亿个HTTP请求进行了为期41个月的纵向分析，发现扩展标识符(EIDs)已广泛普及（覆盖83.76%的网站），但存在准确性不足、隐私风险高以及合规性问题，表明其尚未准备好替代第三方Cookie追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着浏览器限制导致第三方Cookie逐渐淘汰，在线广告生态系统正转向使用扩展标识符(EIDs)作为替代方案。EIDs是持久的用户标识符（如哈希电子邮件地址），用于跨域和跨设备链接用户。然而，目前缺乏对EIDs在实际生态系统中使用情况、准确性和隐私影响的大规模纵向研究。

Method: 对超过1.45亿个HTTP header bidding请求进行41个月的纵向研究，这些请求来自616,539个网站并发送到六个主要供应方平台(SSP)。分析了18个流行EID提供商（占数据集中所有传输EID的99.42%）的使用模式、准确性和隐私实践。

Result: (1) EIDs广泛普及，到2025年5月覆盖83.76%的研究网站；(2) 准确性问题：仅一个提供商能一致识别自标识的爬虫机器人，许多提供商为同一访问者传输多个EID；(3) 隐私风险：12个提供商创建持久性EID，可跨访问、网站、设备和月份识别同一用户；(4) 合规问题：16个提供商在未经用户同意的情况下向欧盟网站传输EID。

Conclusion: EIDs作为第三方Cookie追踪的替代方案尚未准备充分，存在准确性不足、隐私保护缺失以及合规性不佳等关键问题，需要进一步改进以满足广告生态系统的需求并保护用户隐私。

Abstract: As third-party cookies fade because of browser restrictions, the online advertising ecosystem is turning to extended identifiers (EIDs) as an alternative. EIDs are persistent user identifiers, such as hashed email addresses, that are employed to link users across domains and devices. This paper presents a 41-month longitudinal study examining EID usage in over 145 million HTTP header bidding requests sent to six major supply-side platforms (SSPs) from 616,539 websites. Our findings show that EIDs are widely used and are becoming increasingly prevalent in the digital advertising ecosystem, reaching 83.76% of studied websites by May 2025. Our analysis of the 18 popular EID providers that account for 99.42% of all transmitted EIDs in our dataset raises concerns about the readiness of EIDs as an alternative to third-party cookie tracking. In terms of accuracy, only one identity provider consistently recognizes and identifies that the visitor is a self-identified bot crawler, and many providers regularly transmit multiple EIDs for the same visitor. We also identify privacy concerns with EIDs, as 12 of the providers create persistent EIDs that can identify the same user across visits, websites, devices, and months. Finally, we found that 16 providers transmit EIDs on EU websites without user consent.

</details>


### [17] [SoK: Where Do Flow Labels Come From? Auditing Label Provenance in Encrypted Traffic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02140)
*Sizhe Huang,Shujie Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文系统化分析了加密流量分类中的标签侧问题，发现现有基准测试存在粗粒度继承和过度严格过滤两种策略缺陷，导致标签不可靠、数据集不透明，且分类准确率受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注模型输入和数据划分的系统化分析，但缺乏对标签侧的系统化审查。标签质量直接影响监督学习效果，而当前基准测试中的标签策略可能存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 审查14个基准测试条目的标签策略；恢复并比对23个引用数据集的原始记录；推导基于侧信道特征的表示相对准确率上限；使用全捕获语料库分析SNI可观测性及共现特征的影响。

Result: 发现两种常见标签策略缺陷：粗粒度继承（标签缺乏证据支撑）和过度严格过滤（仅保留自证流量，丢弃相关流量）；无基准测试提供可计数的选择前总体；23个引用数据集中8个的任务对象与恢复记录不一致；继承型基准的平衡准确率上限仅为0.56-0.76；仅24.95%的连接携带可观测SNI，但丢弃的连接通过共现特征可将宏准确率从0.44提升至0.65。

Conclusion: 当前加密流量分类基准存在标签策略缺陷、数据集不透明、分类性能受限等问题；建议基准构建者和使用者明确选择前总体、合理设计标签策略、充分利用共现特征等。

Abstract: Encrypted traffic classification infers semantics beyond the flow record from transport-layer observables, and supervised training rests on labels that hold for the individual flow they are attached to. Recent systematizations scrutinize model in- puts and data splits; we systematize the complementary label side. Across 14 audited benchmark entries, we identify two recurring label-side strategies: coarse inheritance, which risks labelling flows the evidence does not cover, and overstrict filtering, which keeps only self-attesting flows and risks dis- carding relevant ones. No audited entry exposes a countable pre-selection population, and the task objects downstream papers attach to the same labels disagree with the recovered record in 8 of 23 referenced cells. Under strict side-channel features we derive a representation-relative ceiling on bal- anced accuracy for any classifier restricted to those features: on the public benchmarks that inherit, it ranges from 0.56 to 0.76. On the filtering side, only 24.95% of connections in our fully captured corpus carry an observable SNI of their own; yet the discarded connections raise macro accuracy from 0.44 to 0.65 through same-run co-occurrence features. We end with recommendations for benchmark builders and users.

</details>


### [18] [WiP: Characterizing and Defending Against Mobile-Agent-Driven MFA Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02154)
*Yimeng Liu,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 移动智能体导致基于密码的多因素认证失效（因子崩溃），本文通过构建攻击流程验证了该威胁，并提出基于运动传感器的Android风险信号来区分人类与智能体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动智能体能够自动解释界面、操作应用并协调跨应用工作流，这种能力挑战了传统密码MFA所假设的"人工中介隔离"，导致有效认证因子在同一个自主环境中被组合，即"因子崩溃"现象，需要探索该威胁的实际影响及防御措施。

Method: 构建模块化管道（modular pipeline）实现10种授权MFA工作流，并与两个单智能体基线对比；开发基于运动传感器的Android风险信号，通过设备运动模式区分人类登录与智能体驱动登录。

Result: 模块化管道成功完成所有10种授权MFA工作流（基线方法仅完成3/10和6/10）；运动信号检测方法在116次实验中达到98.4%（桌面放置）和92.6%（手持）的准确率，有效区分人类与智能体登录行为。

Conclusion: 实验验证了因子崩溃威胁的可行性，证明了物理交互感知可作为用户存在的补充证据，为未来MFA设计提供了新的防御思路。

Abstract: Mobile agents automate smartphone tasks by interpreting interfaces, interacting with apps, and coordinating cross-app workflows. This capability challenges the human-mediated separation assumed by passcode-based MFA, creating factor collapse: valid authentication factors are combined within one autonomous environment. Our modular pipeline com- pletes all 10 authorized MFA workflows, compared with 3/10 and 6/10 for two single-agent baselines. We also de- velop a motion-based Android risk signal that distinguishes human- from agent-driven logins with 98.4% table-top and 92.6% hand-held accuracy across 116 pilot sessions. These results demonstrate factor collapse and motivate physical- interaction sensing as complementary evidence of user pres- ence.

</details>


### [19] [Connectivity of HAPS-Based Solutions for Large-Scale Wireless Networks: A Percolation Theory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02536)
*Hao Lin,Mustafa A Kishk,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于高空平台站(HAPS)的三种无线通信方案(H2D、H2G2D和混合方案)，利用渗透理论分析大规模连续网络覆盖的可行性，证明了存在相变现象，并给出了临界条件的上下界以降低部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G时代，森林、河流、海洋和道路网络等难以部署光缆的区域需要大规模连续的互联网服务以支持环境监测、智能农业、远程教育等应用。高空平台站(HAPS)作为解决方案应运而生，可在无光纤区域提供低延迟、高容量服务并构建垂直异构网络(vHetNets)。

Method: 利用渗透理论(percolation theory)研究三种HAPS方案的可行性：HAPS到设备(H2D)方案、HAPS到网关到设备(H2G2D)方案和混合方案。以渗透概率作为关键性能指标，分析亚临界和超临界情况，研究相变现象。

Result: 证明了当HAPS密度或网关密度增加时，渗透概率从零到非零的相变现象出现。数值结果验证了相变临界条件曲线存在于推导的下界和上界之间。

Conclusion: 研究结果可以帮助降低基于HAPS的垂直异构网络解决方案的前期部署成本，为无光纤区域的大规模连续网络覆盖提供理论指导和工程参考。

Abstract: In the era of sixth-generation (6G) wireless communication, numerous applications are expected to be realized, including environmental monitoring, smart agriculture, remote education, security protection, and intelligent transportation systems. These scenarios require large-scale, continuous Internet services in forests, rivers, oceans, and road networks, to name a few, where optical cables are difficult to deploy. High-altitude platform stations (HAPSs) emerge as a promising solution, offering low-latency, high-capacity services while facilitating the establishment of vertical heterogeneous networks (vHetNets) in fiber-less areas. This article investigates three HAPS-based solutions, where HAPSs can serve wireless devices directly or via gateway (GW) networks: the HAPS-to-device (H2D) scheme, the HAPS-to-GW-to-device (H2G2D) scheme, and the hybrid scheme. Leveraging percolation theory, we study the feasibility of large-scale continuous Internet coverage, where the key performance indicator (KPI) is the percolation probability. We discuss the subcritical and supercritical cases in different coverage schemes, and prove that the phase transition from zero to nonzero percolation probability appears when increasing the HAPS density or GW density. Numerical results verify that the curve of the critical condition of the phase transition exists between the derived lower bound and upper bound, which can help reduce the upfront cost of HAPS-based vHetNet solutions.

</details>


### [20] [Integrating Wi-Fi into 3GPP 5G Network Slicing: An Experimental Prototype Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02625)
*Nelson Ion de Oliveira,Marília Costa Muniz,William M. C. do Nascimento,João Pedro Brasil,Victor Farias Monteiro,Sérgio Barros,Maykon Silva,Augusto Venâncio Neto e Vicente A. de Sousa*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文实现了首个集成5G独立组网与Wi-Fi的端到端网络切片原型系统，通过适配TNGF功能扩展切片至WLAN，实验验证了多无线接入技术切片的可行性与动态管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然3GPP标准已定义蜂窝网络的切片架构，但将Wi-Fi等非3GPP技术无缝集成到统一的切片实例中仍缺乏实证研究，特别是在端到端原型验证方面存在空白。

Method: 基于开源5G核心网搭建功能性测试平台，适配可信非3GPP网关功能(TNGF)将网络切片扩展至WLAN网络，实现Wi-Fi传输资源的统一管理，验证多无线接入技术(multi-RAT)切片操作。

Result: 实验结果表明，在不同带宽分配和流量引导策略下，多RAT切片具有动态可行性，为统一的3GPP与非3GPP服务交付提供了具体的概念验证。

Conclusion: 本研究为5G及未来网络中3GPP与非3GPP技术的统一切片管理提供了可行的技术方案和实验依据，推动了异构网络融合切片技术的发展。

Abstract: Network Slicing (NS) is a fundamental pillar of 5G and beyond networks, enabling the provisioning of isolated, logical networks tailored to specific Quality of Service (QoS) requirements. While 3GPP standards comprehensively define slicing architectures over cellular access networks, the seamless integration of Non-3GPP technologies such as Wi-Fi into a unified slice instance remains an active area of investigation, particularly regarding empirical validation. This paper presents an end-to-end prototyping study that integrates 5G Standalone (SA) and Wi-Fi networks by adapting the Trusted Non-3GPP Gateway Function (TNGF) to extend NS to WLAN networks, enabling the unified management of Wi-Fi transmission resources. We implement a functional testbed leveraging an open-source 5G Core and an explicit Non-3GPP access to validate multi-Radio Access Technology (multi-RAT) slice operation. Our empirical results showcase the dynamic viability of multi-RAT slicing under varying bandwidth allocations and traffic steering policies, providing a concrete proof of concept for unified 3GPP and Non-3GPP service delivery.

</details>


### [21] [Microcell Hot Spot and Smart Antennas Evaluation in WCDMA Macrocell System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02636)
*Carlos H. M. Lima,Francisco R. P. Cavalcanti,Vicente A. de Sousa,Emanuel B. Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文分析WCDMA宏蜂窝系统中嵌入热点的分层小区结构，比较微蜂窝天线与智能天线两种解决方案在不同业务场景下的SIR性能增益，为热区流量管理提供最优方案选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WCDMA宏蜂窝系统中存在热点区域，需要有效的流量管理解决方案。研究旨在评估引入智能天线技术对分层小区结构的影响，并对比微蜂窝部署与智能天线技术在解决热点流量问题上的性能差异。

Method: 采用系统级仿真方法，针对热区流量问题设计两种解决方案：微蜂窝天线部署和宏蜂窝智能天线技术。定义不同业务类用户比例的多种场景，评估各方案的信干比(SIR)相对增益。

Result: 通过仿真分析获得了两种解决方案在不同业务场景下的SIR性能增益数据，明确了各方案在不同用户服务类比例场景下的性能表现差异。

Conclusion: 针对不同业务类用户比例的场景，给出了最优解决方案建议，为WCDMA分层蜂窝系统中热点区域的流量管理提供了技术选择依据。

Abstract: The purpose of this contribution is to analyze a hierarchical cell structure with a hot spot embedded in a WCDMA macrocellular system. Particular attention is paid to the impact of introducing smart antennas in this context. A system-level simulation approach is employed, in which we consider two main solutions to address hot-spot traffic: the placement of a microcell antenna or the adoption of smart antenna technology at the macrocell. Relative gains in signal-to-interference ratio (SIR) are then evaluated. Distinct scenarios are defined based on the proportion of users of different service classes. An evaluation of which solution is best suited to each scenario is then conducted.

</details>

### [22] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WMLLM框架，利用大语言模型作为世界模型来预测优化方向，在黑盒优化任务特别是多目标分子优化上实现了最先进的样本效率和优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化问题面临大型、弱结构化、高维搜索空间的挑战，现有方法依赖直接候选生成或试错式精炼，样本效率低下。作者观察到利用世界建模可以提前识别有前景的优化方向，而大语言模型凭借其隐式知识能够非平凡地预测候选结果，这为改进搜索效率提供了可能。

Method: 提出WMLLM自演化优化智能体框架，采用"预测-行动"的世界建模范式。智能体首先预测有前景的方向，然后采取行动生成候选。该方法结合了智能体式多轮精炼、基于种群的搜索和强化学习，在搜索过程中同时精炼隐式世界模型和优化策略。

Result: 在黑盒优化任务上验证了方法的有效性，特别是在多目标分子优化基准测试中，WMLLM在有限的评估预算下达到了最先进的结果，显著提升了样本效率和最终优化性能。

Conclusion: 通过将大语言模型的隐式知识作为世界模型进行预测式优化，WMLLM成功解决了黑盒优化中的样本效率问题，为复杂优化问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [23] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiDrive框架，通过风险感知分层扩散架构和3DICE策略优化，解决了自动驾驶离线强化学习中的分布偏移、风险信号处理和分布外动作生成等问题，在CARLA复杂交通场景中达到85%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型能有效捕获自动驾驶的多模态行为先验，但离线强化学习策略仍面临分布偏移、重尾风险信号、分布外（OOD）动作生成以及高维状态冗余等关键挑战，这些问题限制了自动驾驶决策的安全性和可靠性。

Method: 提出DiDrive框架，包含两个协同组件：（1）风险感知分层扩散（RHDif）架构：在状态空间中通过低级风险门控编码器和高级上下文调制器过滤环境冗余，聚焦安全关键威胁；（2）3DICE策略优化范式：在动作空间中通过样本内校准引导、时空优化和集成候选排序，缓解OOD过高估计和梯度振荡问题。

Result: 在CARLA基准测试中，DiDrive在包含60辆车的复杂高密度交通场景下表现出色，成功率达到85%，平均奖励为4295.68，显著优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线方法。

Conclusion: DiDrive为安全自动驾驶决策提供了一条稳健的技术路径，证明了分布引导的离线扩散框架在处理复杂交通场景中的有效性，特别是在安全关键威胁识别和OOD动作抑制方面取得了重要进展。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [24] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在提示空间中对冻结大语言模型进行用户个性化适配的方法。通过Muse方法进行元学习，发现元目标无法区分用户-支持数据的正确对应关系，导致"元目标崩溃"现象，即模型优化的是通用指令质量而非可迁移的用户适配能力。在两个标准基准测试中，Muse未能显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LLM个性化适配框架化为提示空间的元学习问题，但很少验证优化的元目标是否编码了可迁移的跨用户适配能力，而非仅仅是通用的指令质量改进。作者旨在探究这一核心问题。

Method: 提出Muse方法：通过反思性提示演化在元训练用户群体上演化单一共享的适配提示，冻结后零样本应用于留出用户。设计三组对照实验：未演化的种子提示、结构破坏的控制组（在用户-支持数据错配下元训练）、不变性预言机控制，以分离学习效应与混淆因素。

Result: 在LaMP-2分类和LaMP-3评分基准上（各200个留出用户），Muse相对未演化种子提示和结构破坏控制组均无显著提升。在评分任务上被简单少样本检索方法显著超越（Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001）。元验证目标对用户-支持对应关系是否正确在统计上不敏感（LaMP-2 p=0.555, LaMP-3 p=0.622）。

Conclusion: 发现"元目标崩溃"现象：元验证目标统计上对用户-支持数据对应关系的真实性不敏感，因此无法优化出可迁移的适配能力，而是奖励指令润色和验证集过拟合。提出的控制组协议可有效分离真实适配学习与这些混淆因素。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection. On two standard personalization benchmarks (LaMP-2 categorization and LaMP-3 rating) over 200 held-out users each, Muse does not significantly improve on its own un-evolved seed prompt or on a structure-broken control that meta-trains on mismatched user-support pairs, and is dominated by plain few-shot retrieval on the rating task (Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001). We attribute these outcomes to a single mechanism, meta-objective collapse: the meta-validation objective is statistically invariant to whether the user-support correspondence is genuine (p=0.555 on LaMP-2, p=0.622 on LaMP-3), so it cannot be optimized into transferable adaptation and instead rewards instruction polish and validation overfitting. The seed-prompt, wrong-support, and invariance-oracle controls form a reusable protocol that separates learned adaptation from these confounds.

</details>


### [25] [Efficient Context-Limited Telescope Bibliography Classification for the WASP-2025 Shared Task Using SciBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01647)
*Madhusudhana Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SciBERT的自动分类方法，用于高效生成望远镜文献目录，在WASP-2025竞赛中排名第一，宏F1分数达0.89，并探讨了文本截断与长上下文模型之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 望远镜文献目录的创建对于评估天文台的科学影响和确保研究的可重复性至关重要，但现有流程主要依赖人工操作，既耗时又耗费资源，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用SciBERT模型对科学论文进行自动分类，将其分为四类：科学、仪器、提及和非望远镜相关。针对严格的上下文长度限制（最大512 tokens）和有限的计算资源，研究分析了截断、分块和长上下文模型等不同策略的效果。

Result: 尽管超过一半的样本超过了token限制，该方法仍取得了0.89的宏F1分数，在WASP-2025排行榜上位居榜首。SciBERT的领域对齐特性使其在截断情况下仍能保持稳健的分类性能。

Conclusion: SciBERT在领域对齐方面的优势使其能够有效应对上下文长度限制，在科学文本分类任务中实现了高效的性能。研究为截断、分块和长上下文模型之间的权衡提供了深入见解，为科学文本处理领域提供了效率前沿的参考。

Abstract: The creation of telescope bibliographies is a crucial part of assessing the scientific impact of observatories and ensuring reproducibility in astronomy. This task involves identifying, categorizing, and linking scientific publications that reference or use specific telescopes. However, this process remains largely manual and resource intensive. In this work, we present an efficient SciBERT-based approach for automatic classification of scientific papers into four categories - science, instrumentation, mention, and not telescope. Despite strict context-length constraints (maximum 512 tokens) and limited compute resources, our approach achieved a macro F1 score of 0.89, ranking at the top of the WASP-2025 leaderboard. We analyze the effect of truncation and show that even with half the samples exceeding the token limit, SciBERT's domain alignment enables robust classification. We discuss trade-offs between truncation, chunking, and long-context models, providing insights into the efficiency frontier for scientific text curation.

</details>


### [26] [CliffRank: A Dual-Branch Framework for Activity-Cliff Ranking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01673)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Peiyu Yang,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CliffRank通过结合绝对活性回归与排序一致性学习，有效解决了活性悬崖（activity-cliff）排序难题，在抗菌肽和小分子数据集上均取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性悬崖排序一直是一个难题，因为局部结构变化可能导致活性差异巨大，而能够解析其底层机制的高质量数据仍然有限，需要更有效地利用现有的活性标签。

Method: 提出CliffRank方法，训练两个并行预测器，结合均方误差（MSE）、阈值化列表损失和成对偏好一致性（PPC），其中PPC在偏好概率空间中对齐相对排序。对于PNA模型，PPC在120个epoch后才被激活（延迟激活策略）。

Result: 在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank配合ESM2-t12实现了最高平均Spearman相关性0.5393和平均Recall@50为21.4。在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank配合PNA（PPC延迟激活）实现了最高平均Spearman相关性0.6890，平均Recall@50为30.4（与ACANet-PNA持平）。PPC存在实际应用限制，非对称初始化改善了MolCLR-GIN的平均性能但非所有目标，延迟PPC策略对不同指标效果不一。

Conclusion: CliffRank在活性悬崖排序任务上表现出色，但PPC策略存在实用边界。未来工作应评估更多目标和抗菌肽系统，开发自适应PPC调度策略，并在条件允许时整合蛋白质或膜环境信息。

Abstract: Activity-cliff ranking remains difficult because local structural changes can cause large activity differences, while high-quality data that resolve the underlying mechanisms remain limited. To use available activity labels more effectively, we combine absolute-activity regression with ranking-consistency learning. CliffRank trains two parallel predictors with mean squared error, a thresholded listwise loss, and Pairwise Preference Consistency (PPC), which aligns relative ordering in the preference-probability space. On three antimicrobial peptide datasets, CliffRank with ESM2-t12 achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.5393 and mean Recall@50 of 21.4, although the leading method varied across individual datasets. On three small-molecule datasets, CliffRank with PNA, where PPC was activated after 120 epochs, achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.6890, while its mean Recall@50 of 30.4 matched that of ACANet-PNA. The PPC results also define its practical limits. Asymmetric initialization improved the MolCLR-GIN averages but did not improve every target. For PNA without pretrained weights, delayed PPC improved selected metrics, but no schedule was best for both mean Spearman correlation and mean Recall@50. Future work should evaluate more targets and antimicrobial peptide systems, develop adaptive PPC schedules, and incorporate protein or membrane context when available.

</details>


### [27] [H3DNAS: Hardware-Aware ONNX-Native 3D Point Cloud Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02684)
*Anchit Mulye,Rhythm Baghel,Sujay Kumar Ingle,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H3DNAS是首个无需源代码的3D点云模型压缩框架，直接在ONNX计算图上操作，在ModelNet40上对PointNet系列模型实现49.1%-65.5%的参数削减和1.29×-1.99×的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano等边缘硬件上部署3D点云模型受限于计算和内存预算，而现有压缩方法需要访问模型原始源代码，无法应用于厂商和模型仓库广泛分发的ONNX二进制文件。

Method: H3DNAS提出三项核心技术：(1)通道依赖图(CDG)将ONNX算子分为四类约束并证明自由参数比例ρf是拓扑不变的压缩上限；(2)两阶段层次搜索策略，通过L1重要性通道选择剪枝、输出保真度零样本排序和GhostConv结构变异；(3)完全基于ONNX图手术的源代码无关压缩管道。

Result: 在ModelNet40数据集上，H3DNAS将PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数量分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，同时实现1.99×、1.29×和1.67×的推理加速，精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: H3DNAS成功实现了首个源代码无关的3D点云模型压缩框架，证明了直接在ONNX图上进行硬件感知模型压缩的可行性，为边缘设备部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud models on edge hardware such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano is severely constrained by compute and memory budgets. Existing compression methods require access to the model's original source code, rendering them inapplicable to the Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) binaries commonly distributed by vendors and model repositories. We present \textbf{H3DNAS}, a hardware-aware model compression framework that operates directly on ONNX computational graphs without requiring original source code, architecture class definition, or gradient access during search. H3DNAS makes three contributions: (1) a \textbf{Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)} that classifies ONNX operators into four constraint classes and formally establishes that the free parameter fraction $ρ_f$ is topological invariant, a provable compression ceiling computable in $\mathcal{O}(|V|+|E|)$; (2) a \textbf{Two-Stage Hierarchical Search} that prunes candidate architectures by $L_1$-importance channel selection, ranks them by output fidelity as a zero-shot label-free proxy, and applies GhostConv structural mutation to Pareto-optimal candidates; and (3) the \textbf{first source-code-free compression pipeline for 3D point cloud models}, operating entirely via ONNX graph surgery with no original architecture definition required. On ModelNet40, H3DNAS reduces the number of parameters in PointNet, PointNet++, and PointMLP by $65.5\%$, $43.2\%$, and $49.1\%$, respectively, while achieving $1.99\times$, $1.29\times$, and $1.67\times$ inference speedups with negligible loss in accuracy. The source code is publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/ClarityLab-Org/h3dnas}.

</details>


### [28] [Sim2Signal: Sim-to-Real Benchmarks for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01676)
*Ferdous Al Rafi,Susrik Mukherjee,Latika Liladhar Dekate,Jennifer Yawa Lavoe,Huaiyuan Yao,Shlok Mohanty,Longchao Da,Xuesong Zhou,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Sim2Signal基准，将Sim-to-Real差距分解为四个MDP组件的差距，评估了18种缓解方法，发现直接迁移会降低性能，但缓解方法的有效性取决于网络和差距设置，估计差距变化的方法比使策略不敏感的方法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在仿真中表现良好，但部署到现实世界时常失败，即Sim-to-Real差距问题。在交通信号控制中，这种差距源于传感、动作执行、交通动态和控制目标等多个方面。然而，这些差距的相对影响和现有缓解方法的可靠性尚不清楚，且缺乏标准基准来系统地测量差距和评估缓解方法。

Method: 提出了Sim2Signal基准，将Sim-to-Real差距分解为观测差距、动作差距、转移差距和奖励差距，分别对应MDP四个组件的不匹配。该基准在统一协议下单独引入每种差距。在33个差距设置和10个基于5个真实地点构建的校准网络上，评估了18种缓解方法和2个基础控制器。

Result: 直接迁移在所有四个差距来源上都会导致性能下降，但下降程度无法预测缓解方法的有效性。缓解方法的有效性强烈依赖于网络和差距设置：除动作差距外，在一种情况下有效的方法可能在另一种情况下失败。最有效的方法通常是估计差距所带来的变化，而不是通过域随机化或不变表示使策略不敏感。

Conclusion: Sim2Signal基准为系统性评估Sim-to-Real差距提供了标准框架。研究表明缓解策略应针对性地估计特定差距的影响，而非采用通用的不变性方法。不同网络和差距场景需要定制化的缓解策略，这为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning achieves strong traffic signal control performance in simulation, yet policies trained in simulators often fail once deployed in the real world, a failure known as the Sim-to-Real gap. When RL is applied to traffic signal control, this gap arises from several sources: sensing, action execution, traffic dynamics, and the control objective. Their relative impact and the reliability of existing Sim-to-Real mitigation methods remain insufficiently understood, and the field lacks a standard benchmark for systematically measuring the gap and evaluating mitigation methods. We present Sim2Signal, a benchmark that decomposes the Sim-to-Real gap into observation, action, transition, and reward gaps, corresponding to mismatches in the four components of the underlying MDP, and induces each gap in isolation under a shared protocol. We evaluate 18 mitigation methods on 2 base controllers, across 33 gap settings and 10 calibrated networks built from 5 real-world locations. We find that direct transfer consistently degrades performance across all four gap sources, but the severity of the degradation does not predict the effectiveness of mitigation. Instead, mitigation effectiveness depends strongly on the network and gap setting: outside the action gap, a method that helps in one case may fail in another. The most effective methods generally estimate what the gap changes, rather than make the policy insensitive through domain randomization or invariant representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Red-Pheonix/Sim2RealTSCBenchMark

</details>


### [29] [A Survey on Self-Improving Test-Time Intelligence: Feedback-Driven Adapting, Learning, and Scaling at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01679)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Yaofo Chen,Zhiquan Wen,Jinwu Hu,Zeshuai Deng,Deyu Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Renjie Chen,Zihao Lian,Shoukai Xu,Gang Dai,Yunbei Zhang,Wei Luo,Yifan Zhang,Mingkui Tan,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了反馈驱动的测试时智能（TTI）统一框架，整合了测试时适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展等方向，为推理阶段的AI系统自我改进研究提供了概念基础和研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在部署时自我改进的能力日益重要。现有研究沿两个方向发展：利用测试信号修改模型状态的方法，以及通过额外推理资源改进预测的方法。然而，这些方向在不同社区中使用不同术语进行研究，难以看清它们之间的联系，亟需统一的视角来整合这些碎片化的研究。

Method: 提出反馈驱动的测试时智能（TTI）作为统一视角，通过该框架整合测试时适应（test-time adaptation）、测试时学习（test-time learning）和测试时扩展（test-time scaling）三个领域，分析它们的区别以及在混合系统中日益增多的重叠。基于此框架，综述视觉、语言、多模态学习、生成模型、机器人和医疗健康等领域的代表性应用。

Result: 建立了连接先前碎片化想法的统一框架，明确了测试时自我改进研究的主要方法范式，涵盖了跨多个领域（视觉、语言、多模态、生成模型、机器人、医疗）的代表性应用和开放挑战。

Conclusion: TTI统一框架为研究推理时自我改进的AI系统提供了连贯的概念基础，有助于促进跨社区的研究合作，并为未来研究提供了清晰的研究路线图。

Abstract: The ability of AI systems to improve their behavior during deployment is becoming increasingly important. As inference moves beyond the static execution of a fixed trained model, a growing body of work studies how models can refine their behavior on the fly by exploiting test-time information and additional computation. These developments have largely evolved along two directions: methods that modify the model's state using test-time signals, and methods that improve predictions through extra inference-time resources such as more sampling and tool use. However, these directions are often studied in separate communities with different terminology, making their connections harder to see. In this survey, we present feedback-driven Test-Time Intelligence (TTI) as a unified perspective for understanding such deployment-time improvement. We use this view to relate test-time adaptation, test-time learning, and test-time scaling, highlighting both their distinctions and their growing overlap in hybrid systems. This unified framework helps connect previously fragmented ideas and provides a clearer conceptual foundation for studying inference-time self-improvement. We review major methodological paradigms, representative applications, and open challenges across vision, language, multimodal learning, generative models, robotics, and healthcare. Our goal is to provide a coherent foundation and research roadmap for the study of self-improving AI systems at test time.

</details>


### [30] [Reinforcement Learning and Rule-Based Peer-to-Peer Pricing in Residential PV-BES Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01680)
*Pablo Benalcazar,Maciej Kalka,Wilian Guamán,Jacek Kamiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了P2P电力交易中基于规则和基于学习的定价机制，发现无储能时规则方法表现更优，有储能时强化学习收益显著提升，且SDR定价优于倍增参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着住宅光伏社区的发展，P2P电力交易成为重要交易模式。然而，不同定价机制的性能和适用性尚不明确，需要系统性比较基于规则和基于学习的定价方法，以指导实际应用选择。

Method: 研究比较了两类定价机制：基于规则的基准方法（包括账单分摊、中间市场利率和供需比定价）和基于强化学习的方法（通过深度Q网络实现，评估了倍增参数化和可学习的SDR定价两种模式）。在无储能和有储能两种配置下，以社区储蓄及辅助财务和运营指标评估性能。

Result: 在无储能配置下，规则基准方法优于最佳RL策略；引入储能后，最佳RL策略的社区储蓄从734.23欧元增至978.52欧元。在所有学习模式和两种配置中，SDR定价均优于倍增参数化方法。收益分配在各家庭间呈现异质性。

Conclusion: 基于规则的定价方法在直接比较中仍具竞争力，储能显著改善基于学习方法的绩效。SDR定价机制在强化学习框架下表现优越，但需关注收益分配的公平性问题。

Abstract: This paper compares rule-based and learning-based pricing mechanisms for peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading in residential photovoltaic communities. The rule-based benchmarks comprise bill-sharing as an ex post allocation mechanism, the mid-market rate, and supply-demand-ratio pricing. The reinforcement-learning (RL) formulation is implemented through a Deep Q-Network and evaluated under multiplier-based and learnable SDR-shaped pricing, with a fixed-parameter SDR variant as a non-learning control. Performance is assessed through community savings together with complementary financial and operational indicators. In the base PV-only configuration, the rule-based benchmarks outperform the best RL policy. With battery energy storage, evaluated for the RL policies only, community savings under the best RL policy increase from EUR 734.23 to EUR 978.52. Across the learning-based modes and in both configurations, SDR-shaped pricing outperforms the multiplier-based parameterization considered. The results indicate that rule-based pricing remains highly competitive wherever the two families are compared directly, and that storage substantially improves the learning-based outcomes under this accounting, while the distribution of benefits remains heterogeneous across households.

</details>


### [31] [CACTUS: Mask-Guided Semantic Clean-Label Backdoors in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02450)
*Chao Feng,Burkhard Stiller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CACTUS是一种针对去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的语义触发后门攻击方法，通过将标签一致的语义对转化为目标导向的表示偏移，在多种模态和聚合规则下实现了高达51.2%的平均攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义触发器在联邦学习中比合成补丁更隐蔽，但在聚合轮次中样本依赖的放置可能削弱后门植入效果。在去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中，拓扑依赖的对等聚合会反复混合本地模型，进一步加剧了这一挑战，使得后门攻击更难持续传播。

Method: CACTUS方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 将标签一致的语义对转化为目标导向的表示偏移；(2) 使用掩码引导的模态特定算子隔离触发器效应并在样本间耦合；(3) 在对等聚合之前，反事实地将偏移应用于干净的非目标嵌入。该方法适用于语音、文本、表格和图像等多种模态。

Result: 在9种聚合规则下的实验表明，当恶意节点比例为30%时，CACTUS在Speech Commands数据集上达到51.2%的九规则平均攻击成功率（ASR）。在四个模态中的三个上，CACTUS取得了所有评估攻击中最高的九规则平均ASR。敏感性分析显示ASR随网络拓扑变化，并随恶意节点比例增加而提高。

Conclusion: CACTUS能够通过反复的DFL聚合传播后门，证明了即使在去中心化联邦学习的复杂拓扑结构和多次模型混合场景下，语义触发器仍然可以有效植入并维持攻击效果，这对联邦学习系统的安全性提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Semantic triggers in federated learning (FL) can be less conspicuous than synthetic patches, but sample-dependent placement may weaken backdoor implantation across aggregation rounds. This challenge is compounded in decentralized FL (DFL), where topology-dependent peer aggregation repeatedly mixes local models. CACTUS converts label-consistent semantic pairs into target-directed representation shifts. Mask-guided, modality-specific operators isolate trigger effects, couple them across samples, and apply the shifts counterfactually to clean non-target embeddings before peer aggregation. Experiments cover speech, text, tabular, and image tasks under nine aggregation rules. With 30\% malicious nodes, CACTUS reaches a nine-rule mean attack success rate (ASR) of 51.2\% on Speech Commands and the highest nine-rule mean ASR among evaluated attacks on three of four modalities. Sensitivity analyses show that ASR varies with network topology and increases with the malicious-node ratio. These results indicate that CACTUS can propagate backdoors through repeated DFL aggregation.

</details>

*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从确定性优化视角重新审视均值中位数估计，提出block-Lp估计器族，通过非凸优化实现了比经典凸方法更优的鲁棒性界，逼近oracle常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典median-of-means估计虽然具有鲁棒性，但其鲁棒性常数无法达到最优的trimmed-block oracle常数。本文旨在突破凸估计器的理论限制，探索非凸方法是否能获得更优的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 1. 分析凸block M-估计器的鲁棒性下界；2. 提出非凸block-Lp估计器族（0<p<1）；3. 研究目标函数的优化景观；4. 结合block-level concentration获得统计保证。

Result: 1. 证明所有凸block M-估计器的鲁棒性常数至少为1/(1-2ε)，无法达到oracle常数1/(1-ε)；2. block-Lp估计器的鲁棒性界随p→0连续逼近oracle常数；3. 在温和分离条件下，全局最小值点与oracle一致；4. 目标函数具有良性景观，无坏盆地；5. 在有限2+δ矩条件下获得次高斯偏差界。

Conclusion: 通过引入非凸block-Lp估计器族，成功突破了凸方法的鲁棒性理论极限，在保持良性优化景观的同时实现了接近最优的鲁棒性保证，为重尾和对抗性污染数据下的鲁棒学习提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: We revisit median-of-means estimation from a deterministic optimization viewpoint and develop a family of block-Lp estimators for robust learning with heavy-tailed and adversarially corrupted data. In a block contamination model with at least a fraction 1 minus epsilon of good blocks, we first show that every convex block M-estimator has worst-case robustness constant at least 1 divided by 1 minus 2 epsilon. This matches the classical median-of-means bound and proves that the trimmed-block oracle constant 1 divided by 1 minus epsilon cannot be attained within the convex class. We then introduce a nonconvex block-Lp family for p between 0 and 1 and derive finite-sample deterministic robustness bounds for all global minimizers. As p decreases from 1 toward 0, these bounds continuously approach the trimmed-block oracle constant. For sufficiently small p, the global minimizers coincide with those of the oracle under a mild separation condition. We also show that the block-Lp objectives have a benign landscape, with all local minima remaining close to the truth and no bad basins. Combining these results with block-level concentration yields sub-Gaussian deviation bounds under finite 2 plus delta moments and high-dimensional extensions to robust mean estimation and sparse regression.

</details>


### [33] [Tri-Band Channel Measurement-Enabled Multi-Layer Digital Twin for Terahertz Wireless Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01699)
*Mingjie Zhu,Ziming Yu,Guangjian Wang,Chong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向太赫兹无线数据中心的多层数字孪生框架，通过三频段测量校准物理孪生，并开发了基于AI的信道孪生，实现了高效的信道重建和实时预测，实验证明该框架在覆盖和干扰分析方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算的快速增长推动了对灵活且高容量数据中心互联的需求。太赫兹通信因其超宽带宽和高空间复用能力，成为未来无线数据中心的有前景解决方案，而数字孪生技术能够实现高效的无线规划和实时优化。
